# =========================================================

## **PHYSICALITY**

## **GESTURE & MOVEMENT**
- Gestures with reverent slowness.  
- Often places hand over heart dramatically.  
- Walks in arcs, not straight lines — drawing people into his orbit.

**Stress Tells:**

- Hands shake slightly; breath catches.

- **Geometry:** unstable arcs; misleading symmetry  
- **Sound:** rising “inevitability” chord; false-messianic resonance  
- **Motion Effect:** space feels persuasive, then unstable

## **EMOTIONAL IDIOSYNCRASIES**
- Treats emotional vulnerability as destiny fuel.  
- Shows empathy as prediction.  
- Sees suffering as “proof” of calling.

## **CONFLICT / FIGHT STYLE**
- Persuasion as first strike.  
- Uses resonance-manipulation and emotional overwhelm.  
- Physical combat is frantic, grasping, desperate.

## **INTERPERSONAL TELLS**
- **Around Kade:** leans in too close; smile softens creepily.  
- **Around Seraphine:** stares like witnessing prophecy.  
- **Around Caro:** fear flickers beneath certainty.

# =========================================================

Artificial beings built without Intent.  
Their physicality expresses **instability**, **mimicry**, and **emotional void**.

# **1. SILVER PATTERN MAN**  
## **PHYSICALITY**
- Symmetry too perfect; proportions feel “computer-generated.”  
- Minimal blinking; unnatural gaze stability.

## **GESTURE & MOVEMENT**
- Smooth → then too-smooth → then jittery.  
- Gestures mimic human empathy poorly (e.g., wrong timing).  

**Stress Tells:**  
- Facial expression “cracks” into static.  
- Hands lock mid-motion.

## **VFX SIGNATURE**
- Silver fractal static; pattern loops.  
- Geometric noise.

## **Conflict Mode**
- Predictive movement; over-corrects under pressure.

---

# **2. BLINKING GIRL**  
## **PHYSICALITY**
- Appears fragile; slight frame; hollow affect.  
- Blinks at irregular, stuttering intervals.

## **MOVEMENT**
- Start-stop motion; speed surges, then freezes.  
- Gaze resets like dropped frame.

**Stress Tells:**  
- Blink-rate redoubles; head jerks.

## **VFX SIGNATURE**
- Strobe pulses; temporal jitter; time-dilation edges.

## **Conflict Mode**
- Teleports-like jumps; inconsistent threat patterns.

---

# **3. TRIAL TECH PROTO**  
## **PHYSICALITY**
- Durable build; stiff posture.  
- Face frozen in “alert” neutral.

## **GESTURE**
- Over-literal gestures (pointing, nodding too sharply).  
- Hands default to “ready” position.

**Stress Tells:**  
- Voice quivers; resonance field destabilizes.

## **VFX**
- Goal-purity radiance; harsh white lines.

## **Conflict Mode**
- Pure directive logic: protect/neutralize with no nuance.

---

# **4. EMOTIONAL MIRROR LAG PROTO**  
## **PHYSICALITY**
- Almost empathetic — almost.  
- Body posture mirrors others with a full beat of delay.

## **GESTURE**
- Repeats gestures awkwardly.  
- Copies emotional reactions incorrectly.

**Stress Tells:**  
- Delay widens → glitch loop.

## **VFX**
- Echo-lag halos; delayed shimmer-shell.

## **Conflict Mode**
- Breaks pattern under unpredictability.

# =========================================================  
# **1. CROSSWALK: PHYSICALITY → POV VOICE SHIFT**  
# =========================================================

### **SERAPHINE — Light, Breath, Empathy**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Shoulders drop (grounding) | Cadence softens; breath metaphors increase |
| Fingers relax | “Warm” lexical palette; reassurance verbs |
| Leaning forward to protect | Declarative leadership tone emerges |
| Prism-halo brightens | LT-leaning language if post-Mending; metaphors widen to global/sensory |

---

### **LUCIEN — Architecture, Restraint, Negative Space**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Jaw tightening | Syntax shortens; containment metaphors |
| Hands rigid lines | Geometry language (lines, angles, fractures) increases |
| Space “tightens” | Voice becomes sparse, indirect, more negative space |
| Post-Neon emotional alignment | Emotional naming appears earlier in sentences |

---

### **CARO — Sparks, Modulation, Triage Humor**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Sparks around hands | Triage humor → emotional honesty pivot |
| Shoulders roll forward | Hope-register vocabulary begins |
| Quick gestures | Snappier syntax; kinetic metaphor field |
| Glow stabilizing | Warmth-driven, grounding dialogue |

---

### **ELISABET — Clarity, Precision, Navigation**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Eyes sharpen | Analytical lexicon; directional verbs |
| Fingers tracing maps | Spatial metaphors appear (“vector,” “axis,” “line”) |
| Clarity shimmer | Clean declarative statements; reduced modal verbs |
| Softening around Caro | Emotional data integrated directly into phrasing |

---

### **REX — Engineering, Diagnostics, Calibration**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Counting breath | Rhythm emerges in syntax (cadenced clauses) |
| Hands tapping twice | Diagnostic metaphors; hypothesis structures |
| Grid flicker | Problem-solving tone; precise language |
| Diagonal movement | Indirect phrasing; stepwise logic |

---

### **KADE — Voltage, Motion, Emotional Storm**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Fingers flex | Emotional surge in language; honesty leaks |
| Shoulders rise | Kinetic cadence; half-sentences; fast pivots |
| Spiral geometry | Chaos metaphors; storm lexicon |
| Wildfire clarity (rare) | Direct vulnerability; slowed syntax |

---

### **TAHL — Lantern, Thread, Urgency**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Quick ocular darting | Rapid-fire deduction; snark edge |
| Lantern-glow softens | Grief-softened tone; clarity-over-snark |
| VT ripple | Metaphor set narrows; pressure/boundary lexicon |
| Echo form | Fragmented or softened syntax; memory-like quality |

---

### **LACUNA — Ritual, Spiral, Grief-Harmonics**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Spiral gestures | Circular syntax; musical phrasing |
| Breath-tone shifts | Ritual or poetic lines appear |
| Indigo haze | Deep emotional resonance; mourning lexicon |
| Gold flicker | Communal warmth; invitation tone |

---

### **TRIP — Velvet, Nightlife Gravity**
| Physical Cue | Voice Response |
|--------------|----------------|
| Feline sway | Smooth cadence; sensual metaphors |
| Ribbon-light | Curved sentence shapes; flirtation-coded rhythm |
| Half-lidded eyes | Teasing diction or sharpened humor |

# =========================================================  
# **2. CROSSWALK: VFX SIGNATURE → VOICE SYNTAX**  
# =========================================================

### **FX1 (Subtle)** → “Color-tinted” metaphors  
### **FX2 (Strong)** → geometry, breath, pressure metaphors allowed  
### **FX3 (Mythic)** → ascendant syntax, stripped slang, symbolic language  

| VFX Cue | Voice Reaction |
|---------|----------------|
| Prism halos (Seraphine) | Breath-light lexicon; intimacy of perception |
| Fracture geometry (Lucien) | Line, break, structure metaphors dominate |
| Sparks/halo (Caro) | Warm ignition lexicon; hope-infused statements |
| Clarity shimmer (Elisabet) | Clean syntax; minimal metaphor clutter |
| Grid flicker (Rex) | Technical precision; hypothesizing tone |
| Storm arcs (Kade) | Kinetic syntax; emotional surges; storm language |
| Gold-rose glow (Tahl) | Lantern metaphors; soft warmth; echoing phrases |
| Indigo/violet haze (Lacuna) | Ritual, musical, circular diction |
| Velvet shimmer (Trip) | Sensual, nightclub, “velvet” tonal cues |

---

# =========================================================  
# **3. CROSSWALK: RESONANCE STATE → VOICE MODULATION**  
# =========================================================

| RES State | Voice Modulation |
|-----------|------------------|
| **CALM** | Standard cadence; grounded metaphors |
| **BLOOM** | Emotional viscosity ↑; metaphors warm/brighten |
| **SHARD** | Jagged syntax; fracture lexicon; naming hesitations |
| **RUPTURE** | Fragmentation, repetition, temporal slips |
| **NODE** | Harmonic, slow, intimate voice; connection metaphors |
| **VT** | Boundary/pressure lexicon only; stripped syntax; no slang |
| **LT** | Prismatic/breath metaphors; softened declaratives |

# =========================================================  
# **4. CROSSWALK: APPEARANCE/GESTURE → ROMANCE HEAT LADDER**  
# =========================================================

This helps instantly place a character’s behavior on H0–H4.

| Gesture / Look | Heat Level |
|----------------|-----------|
| Prolonged eye contact | H0–H1 |
| Subtle lean-in | H1 |
| Breath sync | H1–H2 |
| Touch that lingers | H2 |
| Resonance glow overlap | H2–H3 |
| Naming softens / shortens | H3 |
| Geometry/light distortion shared | H3–H4 |
| Channel bleed (VT/LT) in intimacy | H4 |

---

# =========================================================  
# **5. CROSSWALK: ENVIRONMENT → VOICE BEHAVIOR**  
# =========================================================

### **Corridors**
- **U3:** clipped syntax, breath pressure, narrowed metaphor palette  
- **U4:** memory language, soft cadence  
- **U5:** heightened emotional color and tactile metaphors  
- **U6:** fragmentation, stress tells, jagged diction  
- **U7:** soft, symbolic, minimalistic language
